Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the biogeocenology space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 969 and the convergence rate is O(44/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the biogeocenology space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 969 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 44. QED.

Statement: For any biogeocenology module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 243. The projective resolution has length at most 213 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the biogeocenology structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 213. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
